	public class TPSARPack
	{
		public struct PackingData
		{
			public double SurfaceArea;

			public double VoidFraction;

			public double PackSize;

			public double Factor;

			public double C1;

			public double C2;

			public double C3;

			internal PackingData(double theSurfaceArea, double theVoidFraction, double thePackSize, double theFactor, double theC1, double theC2, double theC3)
			{
				this = default(PackingData);
				SurfaceArea = theSurfaceArea;
				VoidFraction = theVoidFraction;
				PackSize = thePackSize;
				Factor = theFactor;
				C1 = theC1;
				C2 = theC2;
				C3 = theC3;
			}

			static PackingData()
			{
			}
		}

		private struct CodeNameDescP
		{
			public int Code;

			public string Name;

			public string Desc;

			internal CodeNameDescP(int theCode, string theName, string theDesc)
			{
				this = default(CodeNameDescP);
				Code = theCode;
				Name = theName;
				Desc = theDesc;
			}

			static CodeNameDescP()
			{
			}
		}

		private struct CodeNameP
		{
			public int Code;

			public string Name;

			internal CodeNameP(int theCode, string theName)
			{
				this = default(CodeNameP);
				Code = theCode;
				Name = theName;
			}

			static CodeNameP()
			{
			}
		}

		private struct NameList
		{
			public string Name;

			public SortedList List;

			internal NameList(string theName, SortedList theList)

				this = default(NameList);
				Name = theName;
				List = theList;
			}

			static NameList()

			}
		}

		{

			{

				Name = theName;
				PackData = thePackData;
			}

			static NamePackingData()
			{
			}
		}

		private class SortDisplayNames : IComparer
		{
			public int Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				DisplayElementPack displayElementPack = (DisplayElementPack)x;
				DisplayElementPack displayElementPack2 = (DisplayElementPack)y;
				return new CaseInsensitiveComparer().Compare(displayElementPack.DisplayText, displayElementPack2.DisplayText);
			}

			int IComparer.Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in Compare
				return this.Compare(x, y);
			}
		}

		private class DisplayElementPack
		{
			private string mvarDisplayText;

			private string mvarDescText;

			private int mvarOptionValue;

			public string DisplayText => mvarDisplayText;

			public string DescText => mvarDescText;

			public int OptionValue => mvarOptionValue;

			public DisplayElementPack()
			{
			}

			public DisplayElementPack(string theText, string theDesc, int theOptionValue)
			{
				mvarDisplayText = theText;
				mvarDescText = theDesc;
				mvarOptionValue = theOptionValue;
			}
		}

		public string PackName
		{
			get
			{
				string result;
				try
				{
					switch (theIndex)
					{
					case 0:
						result = "";
						goto end_IL_0001;
					case -999:
						theIndex = mvarPackType;
						break;
					}
					object obj = mvarPackTypes[theIndex];
					result = ((obj != null) ? ((CodeNameDescP)obj) : default(CodeNameDescP)).Name;
					end_IL_0001:;
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
					Exception ex2 = ex;
					result = "";
					ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

						theIndex = mvarVendor;
						break;
					}
					object obj = mvarVendors[theIndex];
					result = ((obj != null) ? ((CodeNameDescP)obj) : default(CodeNameDescP)).Desc;
					end_IL_0001:;
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
					Exception ex2 = ex;
					result = "";
					ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public string MaterialName
		{
			get
			{
				string result;
				try
				{
					switch (theIndex)
					{
					case 0:
						result = "";
						goto end_IL_0001;
					case -999:
						theIndex = mvarMaterial;
						break;
					}
					object obj = mvarMaterials[theIndex];
					result = ((obj != null) ? ((CodeNameP)obj) : default(CodeNameP)).Name;
					end_IL_0001:;
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
					Exception ex2 = ex;
					result = "";
					ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public string PackSizeName
		{
			get
			{
				string result;
				try
				{
					switch (theIndex)
					{
					case 0:
						result = "";
						goto end_IL_0001;
					case -999:
						theIndex = mvarPackSize;
						break;
					}
					object obj = mvarPackSizes[theIndex];
					result = ((obj != null) ? ((CodeNameP)obj) : default(CodeNameP)).Name;
					end_IL_0001:;
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
					Exception ex2 = ex;
					result = "";
					ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public PackingData PackData => mvarPackData;

		public bool IsDefined => (mvarPackType != 0) & (mvarVendor != 0) & (mvarMaterial != 0) & (mvarPackSize != 0);

		public int PackType
		{
			get
			{
				return mvarPackType;
			}
			set
			{
				if (mvarPackType != value)
				{
					mvarPackType = value;
					RefreshDisplayLists();
				}
			}
		}

		public int Vendor
		{
			get
			{
				return mvarVendor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (mvarVendor != value)
				{
					mvarVendor = value;
					RefreshDisplayLists();
				}
			}
		}

		public int Material
		{
			get
			{
				return mvarMaterial;
			}
			set
			{
				if (mvarMaterial != value)
				{
					mvarMaterial = value;
					RefreshDisplayLists();
				}
			}
		}

		public int PackSize
		{
			get
			{
				return mvarPackSize;
			}
			set
			{
				if (mvarPackSize != value)
				{
					mvarPackSize = value;
					RefreshDisplayLists();
				}
			}
		}

		public ArrayList DisplayPDropMethods => mvarDisplayPDropMethods;

		public ArrayList DisplayPackings => mvarDisplayPackings;

		public ArrayList DisplayVendors => mvarDisplayVendors;

		public ArrayList DisplayMaterials => mvarDisplayMaterials;

		public ArrayList DisplayPackSizes => mvarDisplayPackSizes;

		public TPSARPack()
		{
			myComparer = new SortDisplayNames();
			mvarPackType = 0;
			mvarVendor = 0;
			mvarMaterial = 0;
			mvarPackSize = 0;
			BuildLists();
			IniDisplayLists();
		}

		~TPSARPack()
		{
			base.Finalize();
		}

		private void BuildLists()
		{
			mvarDisplayPDropMethods = new ArrayList();
			ArrayList arrayList = mvarDisplayPDropMethods;
			arrayList.Clear();
			arrayList.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
			checked
			{
				int num = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfDPMethodForPacking() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpDPMethod = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getPackDPMethodByIndex(i, ref lpDPMethod) == 0 && Operators.CompareString(lpDPMethod.name, "USER", TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						arrayList.Add(new DisplayElementPack(lpDPMethod.name, lpDPMethod.desc, lpDPMethod.code));
					}
				}
				arrayList = null;
				mvarDisplayPDropMethods.Sort(myComparer);
				mvarPackTypes = new SortedList();
				mvarVendors = new SortedList();
				mvarMaterials = new SortedList();
				mvarPackSizes = new SortedList();
				mvarPackings = new SortedList();
				int numberOfPackTypes = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfPackTypes();
				int nVendors = default(int);
				int nMaterials = default(int);
				int nSizes = default(int);
				for (int j = 0; j <= numberOfPackTypes - 1; j++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpPackInfo = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getPackTypeByIndex(j, ref lpPackInfo) != 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (!mvarPackTypes.Contains(lpPackInfo.code))
					{
						mvarPackTypes.Add(lpPackInfo.code, new CodeNameDescP(lpPackInfo.code, lpPackInfo.name, lpPackInfo.desc));
					}
					if (ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfVendors(ref lpPackInfo, ref nVendors) != 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					for (int k = 0; k <= nVendors - 1; k++)
					{
						ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpVendorInfo = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
						if (ATTPSARLib.getVendorByIndex(ref lpPackInfo, k, ref lpVendorInfo) != 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (!mvarVendors.Contains(lpVendorInfo.code))
						{
							mvarVendors.Add(lpVendorInfo.code, new CodeNameDescP(lpVendorInfo.code, lpVendorInfo.name, lpVendorInfo.desc));
						}
						if (ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfMaterialsC(ref lpPackInfo, ref lpVendorInfo, ref nMaterials) != 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						for (int l = 0; l <= nMaterials - 1; l++)
						{
							ATTPSARLib.CodeName lpMaterialInfo = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeName);
							if (ATTPSARLib.getMaterialByIndexC(ref lpPackInfo, ref lpVendorInfo, l, ref lpMaterialInfo) != 0)
							{
								continue;
							}
							if (!mvarMaterials.Contains(lpMaterialInfo.code))
							{
								mvarMaterials.Add(lpMaterialInfo.code, new CodeNameP(lpMaterialInfo.code, lpMaterialInfo.name));
							}
							if (ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfPackSizesC(ref lpPackInfo, ref lpVendorInfo, ref lpMaterialInfo, ref nSizes) != 0)
							{
								continue;
							}
							if (nSizes > 0)
							{
								for (int m = 0; m <= nSizes; m++)
								{
									ATTPSARLib.CodeName lpSizeInfo = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeName);
									if (ATTPSARLib.getPackSizeByIndexC(ref lpPackInfo, ref lpVendorInfo, ref lpMaterialInfo, m, ref lpSizeInfo) == 0)
									{
										if (!mvarPackSizes.Contains(lpSizeInfo.code))
										{
											mvarPackSizes.Add(lpSizeInfo.code, new CodeNameP(lpSizeInfo.code, lpSizeInfo.name));
										}
										ATTPSARLib.PackingParam lpPackData = default(ATTPSARLib.PackingParam);
										if (ATTPSARLib.FillPackingParamsC(bInitParams: true, bInitCodes: false, ref lpPackData, ref lpPackInfo, ref lpVendorInfo, ref lpMaterialInfo, ref lpSizeInfo) == 0)
										{
											AddPackingData(lpPackInfo.code, lpPackInfo.name, lpVendorInfo.code, lpVendorInfo.name, lpMaterialInfo.code, lpMaterialInfo.name, lpSizeInfo.code, lpSizeInfo.name, new PackingData(lpPackData.SpecificSurfaceArea, lpPackData.VoidFraction, lpPackData.NominalDiameter, lpPackData.PackingFactor, lpPackData.StichlmairC1, lpPackData.StichlmairC2, lpPackData.StichlmairC3));
										}
									}
								}
							}
							else
							{
								if (!mvarPackSizes.Contains(9999))
								{
									mvarPackSizes.Add(9999, new CodeNameP(9999, "STANDARD"));
								}
								AddPackingData(lpPackInfo.code, lpPackInfo.name, lpVendorInfo.code, lpVendorInfo.name, lpMaterialInfo.code, lpMaterialInfo.name, 99999, "STANDARD", new PackingData(1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 3.0, 2.1, 0.31));
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddPackingData(int thePackingCode, string thePackingName, int theVendorCode, string theVendorName, int theMaterialCode, string theMaterialName, int thePackSizeCode, string thePackSizeName, PackingData thePackData)
		{
			if (!mvarPackings.ContainsKey(thePackingCode))
			{
				mvarPackings.Add(thePackingCode, new NameList(thePackingName, new SortedList()));
			}
			object obj = mvarPackings[thePackingCode];
			NameList nameList = ((obj != null) ? ((NameList)obj) : default(NameList));
			if (!nameList.List.ContainsKey(theVendorCode))
			{
				nameList.List.Add(theVendorCode, new NameList(theVendorName, new SortedList()));
			}
			object obj2 = nameList.List[theVendorCode];
			NameList nameList2 = ((obj2 != null) ? ((NameList)obj2) : default(NameList));
			if (!nameList2.List.ContainsKey(theMaterialCode))
			{
				nameList2.List.Add(theMaterialCode, new NameList(theMaterialName, new SortedList()));
			}
			object obj3 = nameList2.List[theMaterialCode];
			NameList nameList3 = ((obj3 != null) ? ((NameList)obj3) : default(NameList));
			if (!nameList3.List.ContainsKey(thePackSizeCode))
			{
				nameList3.List.Add(thePackSizeCode, new NamePackingData(thePackSizeName, thePackData));
			}
		}

		private void IniDisplayLists()
		{
			mvarDisplayPackings = new ArrayList();
			mvarDisplayPackings.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
			foreach (object key in mvarPackTypes.GetKeyList())
			{
				object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(key);
				object obj = mvarPackings[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue)];
				DisplayElementPack value = new DisplayElementPack(((obj != null) ? ((NameList)obj) : default(NameList)).Name, "", Conversions.ToInteger(objectValue));
				mvarDisplayPackings.Add(value);
			}
			mvarDisplayPackings.Sort(myComparer);
			mvarDisplayVendors = new ArrayList();
			mvarDisplayVendors.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
			mvarDisplayMaterials = new ArrayList();
			mvarDisplayMaterials.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
			mvarDisplayPackSizes = new ArrayList();
			mvarDisplayPackSizes.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
			mvarPackData = new PackingData(1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35);
		}

		public void ResetSelections()
		{
			mvarDisplayPackings = new ArrayList();
			mvarDisplayPackings.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
			foreach (object key in mvarPackTypes.GetKeyList())
			{
				object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(key);
				object obj = mvarPackings[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue)];
				DisplayElementPack value = new DisplayElementPack(((obj != null) ? ((NameList)obj) : default(NameList)).Name, "", Conversions.ToInteger(objectValue));
				mvarDisplayPackings.Add(value);
			}
			mvarDisplayPackings.Sort(myComparer);
			mvarPackType = 0;
			mvarDisplayVendors = new ArrayList();
			mvarDisplayVendors.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
			mvarVendor = 0;
			mvarDisplayMaterials = new ArrayList();
			mvarDisplayMaterials.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
			mvarMaterial = 0;
			mvarDisplayPackSizes = new ArrayList();
			mvarDisplayPackSizes.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
			mvarPackSize = 0;
			mvarPackData = new PackingData(1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35);
		}

		private void RefreshDisplayLists()
		{
			if (mvarPackings.ContainsKey(mvarPackType))
			{
				object obj = mvarPackings[mvarPackType];
				mvarPackingVendors = ((obj != null) ? ((NameList)obj) : default(NameList));
				mvarDisplayVendors = new ArrayList();
				mvarDisplayVendors.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
				foreach (object key in mvarPackingVendors.List.GetKeyList())
				{
					object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(key);
					object obj2 = mvarPackingVendors.List[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue)];
					DisplayElementPack value = new DisplayElementPack(((obj2 != null) ? ((NameList)obj2) : default(NameList)).Name, "", Conversions.ToInteger(objectValue));
					mvarDisplayVendors.Add(value);
				}
				mvarDisplayVendors.Sort(myComparer);
				if (mvarPackingVendors.List.ContainsKey(mvarVendor))
				{
					object obj3 = mvarPackingVendors.List[mvarVendor];
					mvarVendorMaterials = ((obj3 != null) ? ((NameList)obj3) : default(NameList));
					mvarDisplayMaterials = new ArrayList();
					mvarDisplayMaterials.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
					foreach (object key2 in mvarVendorMaterials.List.GetKeyList())
					{
						object objectValue2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(key2);
						object obj4 = mvarVendorMaterials.List[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue2)];
						DisplayElementPack value2 = new DisplayElementPack(((obj4 != null) ? ((NameList)obj4) : default(NameList)).Name, "", Conversions.ToInteger(objectValue2));
						mvarDisplayMaterials.Add(value2);
					}
					mvarDisplayMaterials.Sort(myComparer);
					if (mvarVendorMaterials.List.ContainsKey(mvarMaterial))
					{
						object obj5 = mvarVendorMaterials.List[mvarMaterial];
						mvarMaterialSizes = ((obj5 != null) ? ((NameList)obj5) : default(NameList));
						mvarDisplayPackSizes = new ArrayList();
						mvarDisplayPackSizes.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
						foreach (object key3 in mvarMaterialSizes.List.GetKeyList())
						{
							object objectValue3 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(key3);
							object obj6 = mvarMaterialSizes.List[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue3)];
							DisplayElementPack value3 = new DisplayElementPack(((obj6 != null) ? ((NamePackingData)obj6) : default(NamePackingData)).Name, "", Conversions.ToInteger(objectValue3));
							mvarDisplayPackSizes.Add(value3);
						}
						mvarDisplayMaterials.Sort(myComparer);
						if (mvarMaterialSizes.List.ContainsKey(mvarPackSize))
						{
							object obj7 = mvarMaterialSizes.List[mvarPackSize];
							mvarPackData = ((obj7 != null) ? ((NamePackingData)obj7) : default(NamePackingData)).PackData;
						}
						else
						{
							mvarPackData = new PackingData(1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						mvarMaterialSizes = default(NameList);
						mvarPackSize = 0;
						mvarDisplayPackSizes = new ArrayList();
						mvarDisplayPackSizes.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
						mvarPackData = new PackingData(1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					mvarVendorMaterials = default(NameList);
					mvarMaterial = 0;
					mvarDisplayMaterials = new ArrayList();
					mvarDisplayMaterials.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
					mvarMaterialSizes = default(NameList);
					mvarPackSize = 0;
					mvarDisplayPackSizes = new ArrayList();
					mvarDisplayPackSizes.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
					mvarPackData = new PackingData(1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				mvarPackingVendors = default(NameList);
				mvarVendor = 0;
				mvarDisplayVendors = new ArrayList();
				mvarDisplayVendors.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
				mvarVendorMaterials = default(NameList);
				mvarMaterial = 0;
				mvarDisplayMaterials = new ArrayList();
				mvarDisplayMaterials.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
				mvarMaterialSizes = default(NameList);
				mvarPackSize = 0;
				mvarDisplayPackSizes = new ArrayList();
				mvarDisplayPackSizes.Add(new DisplayElementPack("", "", 0));
				mvarPackData = new PackingData(1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35);
			}
		}
	}
	public class TPSARTray
	{
		private class DisplayElementTray
		{
			private string mvarDisplayText;

			private string mvarDescText;

			private int mvarOptionValue;

			private double mvarOptionValueAlt;

			public string DisplayText => mvarDisplayText;

			public string DescText => mvarDescText;

			public int OptionValue => mvarOptionValue;

			public int OptionValueAlt => checked((int)Math.Round(mvarOptionValueAlt));

			public DisplayElementTray()
			{
			}

			public DisplayElementTray(string theText, string theDescText, int theOptionValue, int theOptionValueAlt)
			{
				mvarDisplayText = theText;
				mvarDescText = theDescText;
				mvarOptionValue = theOptionValue;
				mvarOptionValueAlt = theOptionValueAlt;
			}
		}

		private ArrayList mvarTrayTypes;

		private ArrayList mvarDeckThicknesses;

		private ArrayList mvarValveTypesGlitsch;

		private ArrayList mvarValveTypesKoch;

		private ArrayList mvarValveTypesNutter;

		private ArrayList mvarValveThicknesses;

		private ArrayList mvarValveMaterials;

		private ArrayList mvarValveLifts;

		private ArrayList mvarEdgeTypes;

		private ArrayList mvarSkirtHeights;

		private ArrayList mvarCapDiameters;

		private ArrayList mvarFloodCalcMethodsKoch;

		private ArrayList mvarFloodCalcMethodsAlt;

		public ArrayList TrayTypes => mvarTrayTypes;

		public ArrayList DeckThicknesses => mvarDeckThicknesses;

		public ArrayList ValveTypes
		{
			get
			{
				switch (theTrayType)
				{
				case 3:
					return mvarValveTypesGlitsch;
				case 4:
					return mvarValveTypesKoch;
				case 5:
					return mvarValveTypesNutter;
				default:
				{
					ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
					arrayList.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
					return arrayList;
				}
				}
			}
		}

		public ArrayList ValveThicknesses
		{
			get
			{
				if (theValveType == 3)
				{
					return mvarValveThicknesses;
				}
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				arrayList.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				return arrayList;
			}
		}

		public ArrayList ValveMaterials
		{
			get
			{
				if (theValveType == 3)
				{
					return mvarValveMaterials;
				}
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				arrayList.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				return arrayList;
			}
		}

		public ArrayList ValveLifts
		{
			get
			{
				if (theValveType == 5)
				{
					return mvarValveLifts;
				}
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				arrayList.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				return arrayList;
			}
		}

		public ArrayList EdgeTypes
		{
			get
			{
				if (theTrayType == 5)
				{
					return mvarEdgeTypes;
				}
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				arrayList.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				return arrayList;
			}
		}

		public ArrayList SkirtHeights => mvarSkirtHeights;

		public ArrayList CapDiameters => mvarCapDiameters;

		public ArrayList FloodCalcMethods
		{
			get
			{
				switch (theTrayType)
				{
				case 1:
				case 2:
				case 3:
					return mvarFloodCalcMethodsAlt;
				case 4:
					return mvarFloodCalcMethodsKoch;
				default:
				{
					ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
					arrayList.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
					return arrayList;
				}
				}
			}
		}

		public TPSARTray()
		{
			BuildLists();
		}

		private void BuildLists()
		{
			mvarTrayTypes = new ArrayList();
			ArrayList arrayList = mvarTrayTypes;
			arrayList.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
			checked
			{
				int num = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfTrayTypes() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getTrayTypeByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) == 0)
					{
						switch (lpTrayType.name)
						{
						case "CAPS":
							lpTrayType.name = "Bubble Cap";
							break;
						case "SIEVE":
							lpTrayType.name = "Sieve";
							break;
						case "BALLAST":
							lpTrayType.name = "Glitsch Ballast";
							break;
						case "FLEXI":
							lpTrayType.name = "Koch Flexitray";
							break;
						case "FLOAT":
							lpTrayType.name = "Nutter Float Valve";
							break;
						}
						DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpTrayType.name, lpTrayType.desc, lpTrayType.code, i);
						arrayList.Add(value);
					}
				}
				arrayList = null;
				mvarDeckThicknesses = new ArrayList();
				ArrayList arrayList2 = mvarDeckThicknesses;
				arrayList2.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				int num2 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfDeckThicknesses() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num2; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getDeckThicknessByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) == 0)
					{
						DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpTrayType.name, lpTrayType.desc, lpTrayType.code, i);
						arrayList2.Add(value);
					}
				}
				arrayList2 = null;
				mvarValveTypesGlitsch = new ArrayList();
				mvarValveTypesGlitsch.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				mvarValveTypesKoch = new ArrayList();
				mvarValveTypesKoch.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				mvarValveTypesNutter = new ArrayList();
				mvarValveTypesNutter.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				int num3 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfValveTypes() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num3; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getValveTypeByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) == 0)
					{
						DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpTrayType.name, lpTrayType.desc, lpTrayType.code, i);
						if (lpTrayType.desc.IndexOf("Glitsch") > -1)
						{
							mvarValveTypesGlitsch.Add(value);
						}
						else if (lpTrayType.desc.IndexOf("Koch") > -1)
						{
							mvarValveTypesKoch.Add(value);
						}
						else if (lpTrayType.desc.IndexOf("Nutter") > -1)
						{
							mvarValveTypesNutter.Add(value);
						}
					}
				}
				mvarValveThicknesses = new ArrayList();
				ArrayList arrayList3 = mvarValveThicknesses;
				arrayList3.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				int num4 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfValveThicknesses() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num4; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getValveThicknessByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) == 0)
					{
						DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpTrayType.name, lpTrayType.desc, lpTrayType.code, i);
						arrayList3.Add(value);
					}
				}
				arrayList3 = null;
				mvarValveMaterials = new ArrayList();
				ArrayList arrayList4 = mvarValveMaterials;
				arrayList4.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				int num5 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfValveMaterials() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num5; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getValveMaterialByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) == 0)
					{
						DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpTrayType.name, lpTrayType.desc, lpTrayType.code, i);
						arrayList4.Add(value);
					}
				}
				arrayList4 = null;
				mvarValveLifts = new ArrayList();
				ArrayList arrayList5 = mvarValveLifts;
				arrayList5.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				int num6 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfValveLifts() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num6; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getValveLiftByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) == 0)
					{
						DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpTrayType.name, lpTrayType.desc, lpTrayType.code, i);
						arrayList5.Add(value);
					}
				}
				arrayList5 = null;
				mvarEdgeTypes = new ArrayList();
				ArrayList arrayList6 = mvarEdgeTypes;
				arrayList6.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				int num7 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfEdgeTypes() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num7; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getEdgeTypeByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) == 0)
					{
						DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpTrayType.name, lpTrayType.desc, lpTrayType.code, i);
						arrayList6.Add(value);
					}
				}
				arrayList6 = null;
				mvarSkirtHeights = new ArrayList();
				ArrayList arrayList7 = mvarSkirtHeights;
				arrayList7.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				int num8 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfSkirtHeights() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num8; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getSkirtHeightByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) == 0)
					{
						DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpTrayType.name, lpTrayType.desc, lpTrayType.code, i);
						arrayList7.Add(value);
					}
				}
				arrayList7 = null;
				mvarCapDiameters = new ArrayList();
				ArrayList arrayList8 = mvarCapDiameters;
				arrayList8.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				int num9 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfCapDiameters() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num9; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getCapDiameterByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) == 0)
					{
						DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpTrayType.name, lpTrayType.desc, lpTrayType.code, i);
						arrayList8.Add(value);
					}
				}
				arrayList8 = null;
				mvarFloodCalcMethodsKoch = new ArrayList();
				mvarFloodCalcMethodsKoch.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				mvarFloodCalcMethodsAlt = new ArrayList();
				mvarFloodCalcMethodsAlt.Add(new DisplayElementTray("", "", 0, 0));
				int num10 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfTrayTypes() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num10; i++)
				{
					ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpTrayType = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
					if (ATTPSARLib.getTrayTypeByIndex(i, ref lpTrayType) != 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (lpTrayType.name.Equals("SIEVE"))
					{
						int num11 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfTrayRatingFloodingMethod(i) - 1;
						for (int j = 0; j <= num11; j++)
						{
							ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpFloodMethod = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
							if (ATTPSARLib.getTrayRatingFloodingMethodByIndex(i, j, ref lpFloodMethod) == 0 && Operators.CompareString(lpFloodMethod.name, "User", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpFloodMethod.name, lpFloodMethod.desc, lpFloodMethod.code, j);
								mvarFloodCalcMethodsAlt.Add(value);
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (!lpTrayType.name.Equals("FLOAT"))
						{
							continue;
						}
						int num12 = ATTPSARLib.getNumberOfTrayRatingFloodingMethod(i) - 1;
						for (int j = 0; j <= num12; j++)
						{
							ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc lpFloodMethod = default(ATTPSARLib.CodeNameDesc);
							if (ATTPSARLib.getTrayRatingFloodingMethodByIndex(i, j, ref lpFloodMethod) == 0 && Operators.CompareString(lpFloodMethod.name, "User", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								DisplayElementTray value = new DisplayElementTray(lpFloodMethod.name, lpFloodMethod.desc, lpFloodMethod.code, j);
								mvarFloodCalcMethodsKoch.Add(value);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	[Guid("4E1D34DC-BD41-4B02-9451-7220465E11E8")]
	[ProgId("BSPCycleManager410.Main")]
	public class Main : IAspenModelerEvents
	{
		public class DebugUtils
		{
			[DllImport("msvcrt.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto, SetLastError = true)]
			public static extern int _controlfp(int IN_New, int IN_Mask);
		}

		private AspenCustomModeler oApp;

		private AspenModelerSimulation oSim;

		private object oFlow;

		private bool bEndBatch;

		private bool bStartBatch;

		private bool bDebugOn;

		private SortedList cReporters;

		private bool bEoS;

		private bool bEoB;

		private bool bEoR;

		private bool bACMInit;

		private bool bInOnNewBlockEvent;

		private bool bDocumentOpen;

		private object oBlock;

		private bool bEventSinkAdded;

		public bool DebugOn
		{
			set
			{
				bDebugOn = value;
			}
		}

		public object Application
		{
			get
			{
				return oApp;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value == null) & (oApp != null))
				{
					oFlow = null;
					oSim = null;
					if (bEventSinkAdded)
					{
						oApp.RemoveEventSink(this);
						bEventSinkAdded = false;
					}
					oApp = null;
					GC.Collect();
				}
				oApp = (AspenCustomModeler)value;
				if (oApp == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				oSim = (AspenModelerSimulation)oApp.Simulation;
				oFlow = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(oSim.Flowsheet);
				if (oSim != null)
				{
					bDocumentOpen = true;
				}
				if (!bEventSinkAdded)
				{
					oApp.AddEventSink(this);
					bEventSinkAdded = true;
				}
				object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(oFlow, null, "Blocks", new object[0], null, null, null));
				foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)objectValue)
				{
					object objectValue2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(item);
					if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue2, null, "TypeName", new object[0], null, null, null), "BatchSep", TextCompare: false))
					{
						EnsureReportFileName(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue2));
						CreateReporter(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue2));
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public object Block
		{
			get
			{
				return RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(oBlock);
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value == null) & (oBlock != null))
				{
					oBlock = null;
					oApp = null;
					GC.Collect();
				}
				oBlock = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(value);
				if (oBlock != null)
				{
					CreateReporter(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(oBlock));
				}
			}
		}

		public Main()
		{
			cReporters = new SortedList();
			bEoS = false;
			bEoB = false;
			bEoR = false;
			bACMInit = false;
			bInOnNewBlockEvent = false;
			bDocumentOpen = false;
			bEventSinkAdded = false;
			bDebugOn = false;
		}

		~Main()
		{
			if (oApp == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (bEventSinkAdded)
				{
					oApp.RemoveEventSink(this);
					bEventSinkAdded = false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			foreach (Reporter value in cReporters.Values)
			{
				value.Dispose();
				Reporter reporter = null;
			}
			cReporters = null;
			oFlow = null;
			oSim = null;
			oApp = null;
		}

		public void Report()
		{
			string text = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(oBlock, null, "currentuserevent", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			foreach (Reporter value in cReporters.Values)
			{
				switch (text)
				{
				case "BSRunEnd":
					value.Report("EoR");
					break;
				case "BSInitCompletedOK":
					value.Report("SoR");
					break;
				case "BSStartNextBatch":
					value.Report("EoB");
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void CloseReport()
		{
			foreach (Reporter value in cReporters.Values)
			{
				value.Dispose();
				Reporter reporter = null;
			}
			cReporters = null;
		}

		private void CreateReporter(object blk)
		{
			try
			{
				if (bInOnNewBlockEvent)
				{
					int num = DebugUtils._controlfp(524319, 16);
					bInOnNewBlockEvent = false;
				}
				Reporter reporter = new Reporter();
				reporter.ACMObject = oApp;
				reporter.ControlContext = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(blk);
				cReporters.Add(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(blk, null, "Name", new object[0], null, null, null)), reporter);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				if (oApp != null)
				{
					oApp.Msg("Error while creating reporter: " + ex2.Message);
					oApp.Msg("                                    " + ex2.StackTrace);
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		private void CreateReporter()
		{
			object obj = null;
			try
			{
				foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(oApp.Simulation, null, "Flowsheet", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "blocks", new object[0], null, null, null))
				{
					obj = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(item);
					if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(NewLateBinding.LateGet(obj, null, "TypeName", new object[0], null, null, null), "BatchSep", TextCompare: false))
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (obj != null)
				{
					EnsureReportFileName(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(obj));
					Reporter reporter = new Reporter();
					reporter.ACMObject = oApp;
					reporter.ControlContext = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(obj);
					cReporters.Add(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(obj, null, "Name", new object[0], null, null, null)), reporter);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				if (oApp != null)
				{
					oApp.Msg("Error while creating reporter: " + ex2.Message);
					oApp.Msg("                                    " + ex2.StackTrace);
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		private void EnsureReportFileName(object blk)
		{
			string text = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(blk, null, "ReportFormat", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			string defaultReportFileName = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(oApp.ActiveDocument, null, "Name", new object[0], null, null, null));
			string text2 = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(oApp.ActiveDocument, null, "Path", new object[0], null, null, null));
			SetDefaultReportFileName(defaultReportFileName);
			string text3 = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(blk, null, "ReportFile", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			string text4 = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(blk, null, "ReportFilePath", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			if (Operators.CompareString(text4, "", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				text4 = Path.GetDirectoryName(text3);
			}
			if (Operators.CompareString(text4, "", TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				if (!Directory.Exists(text4))
				{
					oApp.Msg("Report path, " + text4 + " does not exist on this machine.\r\nResetting path to problem location");
					NewLateBinding.LateSetComplex(NewLateBinding.LateGet(blk, null, "ReportFilePath", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[1] { "" }, null, null, OptimisticSet: false, RValueBase: true);
				}
				else if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectNotEqual(text4, NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(blk, null, "ReportFilePath", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null), TextCompare: false))
				{
					NewLateBinding.LateSetComplex(NewLateBinding.LateGet(blk, null, "ReportFilePath", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[1] { text4 }, null, null, OptimisticSet: false, RValueBase: true);
				}
				if (Operators.CompareString(text3, Path.GetFileName(text3), TextCompare: false) != 0)
				{
					NewLateBinding.LateSetComplex(NewLateBinding.LateGet(blk, null, "ReportFile", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[1] { Path.GetFileName(text3) }, null, null, OptimisticSet: false, RValueBase: true);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetDefaultReportFileName(string sPath)
		{
			string fileNameWithoutExtension = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(sPath);
			oSim = (AspenModelerSimulation)oApp.Simulation;
			oFlow = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(oSim.Flowsheet);
			object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(oFlow, null, "Blocks", new object[0], null, null, null));
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)objectValue)
			{
				object objectValue2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(item);
				if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue2, null, "TypeName", new object[0], null, null, null), "BatchSep", TextCompare: false))
				{
					string text = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue2, null, "ReportFormat", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
					string text2 = Path.ChangeExtension(fileNameWithoutExtension, text.ToLower());
					NewLateBinding.LateSetComplex(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue2, null, "DefaultReportFile", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[1] { text2 }, null, null, OptimisticSet: false, RValueBase: true);
				}
			}
		}

		private void IAspenModelerEvents_OnDeletedBlock(object sBlockName)
		{
			Reporter reporter = (Reporter)cReporters[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(sBlockName)];
			cReporters.Remove(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(sBlockName));
			reporter?.Dispose();
			reporter = null;
		}

		private void IAspenModelerEvents_OnHasQuit()
		{
			if (oApp == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (bDebugOn)
			{
				oApp.Msg("--Cycle Manager: Destroy Cycle Manager on Quit");
			}
			try
			{
				if (bEventSinkAdded)
				{
					oApp.RemoveEventSink(this);
					bEventSinkAdded = false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				if (oApp != null)
				{
					oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager (OnHasQuit): " + ex2.Message);
					oApp.Msg("                                    " + ex2.StackTrace);
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			try
			{
				foreach (Reporter value in cReporters.Values)
				{
					value.Dispose();
					Reporter reporter = null;
				}
				cReporters = null;
			}
			catch (Exception ex3)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex3);
				Exception ex4 = ex3;
				if (oApp != null)
				{
					oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager Reporting (OnHasQuit): " + ex4.Message);
					oApp.Msg("                                              " + ex4.StackTrace);
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			oFlow = null;
			oSim = null;
			oApp = null;
		}

		void IAspenModelerEvents.OnHasQuit()
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in IAspenModelerEvents_OnHasQuit
			this.IAspenModelerEvents_OnHasQuit();
		}

		private void IAspenModelerEvents_OnNew()
		{
			if (oApp == null)
			{
				return;
			}

			{
				oApp.Msg("--Cycle Manager: Destroy Cycle Manager on New");
			}
			try
			{
				if (bEventSinkAdded)
				{
					oApp.RemoveEventSink(this);
					bEventSinkAdded = false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				if (oApp != null)
				{
					oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager (OnNew): " + ex2.Message);
					oApp.Msg("                                " + ex2.StackTrace);
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			try
			{
				if (cReporters != null)
				{
					foreach (Reporter value in cReporters.Values)
					{
						value.Dispose();
						Reporter reporter = null;
					}
					cReporters = null;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex3)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex3);
				Exception ex4 = ex3;
				if (oApp != null)
				{
					oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager Reporting (OnNew): " + ex4.Message);
					oApp.Msg("                                          " + ex4.StackTrace);
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			oFlow = null;
			oSim = null;
			oApp = null;
		}

		private void IAspenModelerEvents_OnNewBlock(object sBlockName)
		{
			bInOnNewBlockEvent = true;
			object flowsheet = oSim.Flowsheet;
			object[] obj = new object[1] { sBlockName };
			object[] array = obj;
			bool[] obj2 = new bool[1] { true };
			bool[] array2 = obj2;
			object obj3 = NewLateBinding.LateGet(flowsheet, null, "resolve", obj, null, null, obj2);
			if (array2[0])
			{
				sBlockName = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(array[0]);
			}
			object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(obj3);
			if (!Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue, null, "TypeName", new object[0], null, null, null), "BatchSep", TextCompare: false))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(objectValue, null, "invoke", new object[1] { "UpdateInternals" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				if (oApp != null)
				{
					oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager OnNewBlock event.");
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			oApp.Msg("In OnNewBlock. Calling CreateReporter");
			EnsureReportFileName(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue));
			CreateReporter(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue));
		}

		void IAspenModelerEvents.OnNewBlock(object sBlockName)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in IAspenModelerEvents_OnNewBlock
			this.IAspenModelerEvents_OnNewBlock(sBlockName);
		}

		private void IAspenModelerEvents_OnOpened(object sPath)
		{
			if (oApp == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (bDebugOn)
			{
				oApp.Msg("--Cycle Manager: Destroy Cycle Manager on Open");
			}
			try
			{
				if (bEventSinkAdded)
				{
					oApp.RemoveEventSink(this);
					bEventSinkAdded = false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				if (oApp != null)
				{
					oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager (OnOpened)");
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			try
			{
				if (cReporters != null)
				{
					foreach (Reporter value in cReporters.Values)
					{
						value.Dispose();
						Reporter reporter = null;
					}
					cReporters = null;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex3)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex3);
				Exception ex4 = ex3;
				if (oApp != null)
				{
					oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager Reporting (OnOpened): " + ex4.Message);
					oApp.Msg("                                             " + ex4.StackTrace);
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			oFlow = null;
			oSim = null;
			oApp = null;
		}

		private void IAspenModelerEvents_OnRunPaused()
		{
			if (!bDocumentOpen)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(oSim.RunMode, "Optimization", TextCompare: false))
			{
				object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(oSim.Optimization());
				if (Conversions.ToBoolean(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue, null, "IsDynamic", new object[0], null, null, null)))
				{
					try
					{
						NewLateBinding.LateCall(oFlow, null, "InvokeLibraryScript", new object[2] { "BatchSep", "MultipleBatchControl" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
						return;
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
						Exception ex2 = ex;
						if (oApp != null)
						{
							oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager (OnRunPaused)");
						}
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			if (bEndBatch)
			{
				if (bEoB)
				{
					foreach (Reporter value in cReporters.Values)
					{
						NewLateBinding.LateCall(value.ControlContext, null, "FinalConditions", new object[0], null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
						value.Report("EoB");
					}
					bEoB = false;
				}
				if (bDebugOn)
				{
					oApp.Msg("--Cycle Manager: Configuring next cycle");
					bool flag = false;
					try
					{
						NewLateBinding.LateCall(oFlow, null, "MultipleBatchControl", new object[0], null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
					}
					catch (Exception ex3)
					{
						ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex3);
						Exception ex4 = ex3;
						flag = true;
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						try
						{
							NewLateBinding.LateCall(oFlow, null, "InvokeLibraryScript", new object[2] { "BatchSep", "MultipleBatchControl" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
						}
						catch (Exception ex5)
						{
							ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex5);
							Exception ex6 = ex5;
							if (oApp != null)
							{
								oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager (OnRunPaused)");
							}
							ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					try
					{
						NewLateBinding.LateCall(oFlow, null, "InvokeLibraryScript", new object[2] { "BatchSep", "MultipleBatchControl" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
					}
					catch (Exception ex7)
					{
						ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex7);
						Exception ex8 = ex7;
						if (oApp != null)
						{
							oApp.Msg("Error in Cycle Manager (OnRunPaused)");
						}
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
					}
				}
			}
			if (!bEoS)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (Reporter value2 in cReporters.Values)
			{
				value2.Report("EoS");
			}
			bEoS = false;
			RestartRun();
		}

		private void IAspenModelerEvents_OnUserEvent(object sUserString)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			try
			{
				switch (sUserString.ToString())
				{
				case "BSStartNextBatch":
					bStartBatch = true;
					if (bDebugOn)
					{
						oApp.Msg("--Cycle Manager: Starting next cycle");
						bool flag2 = false;
						try
						{
							NewLateBinding.LateCall(oFlow, null, "OnStartRun", new object[0], null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
						}
						catch (Exception ex)
						{
							ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
							Exception ex2 = ex;
							flag2 = true;
							ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						}
						if (!flag2)
						{
							break;
						}
						try
						{
							NewLateBinding.LateCall(oFlow, null, "InvokeLibraryScript", new object[2] { "BatchSep", "OnStartRun" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
							break;
						}
						catch (Exception ex3)
						{
							ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex3);
							Exception ex4 = ex3;
							if (oApp != null)
							{
								oApp.Msg(Operators.ConcatenateObject("Error in Cycle Manager (OnUserEvent): ", sUserString));
							}
							ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
							break;
						}
					}
					try
					{
						NewLateBinding.LateCall(oFlow, null, "InvokeLibraryScript", new object[2] { "BatchSep", "OnStartRun" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
						break;
					}
					catch (Exception ex5)
					{
						ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex5);
						Exception ex6 = ex5;
						if (oApp != null)
						{
							oApp.Msg(Operators.ConcatenateObject("Error in Cycle Manager (OnUserEvent): ", sUserString));
						}
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						break;
					}
				case "BSStepNextBatch":
					bStartBatch = true;
					if (bDebugOn)
					{
						oApp.Msg("--Cycle Manager: Stepping next cycle");
						bool flag2 = false;
						try
						{
							NewLateBinding.LateCall(oFlow, null, "OnStepRun", new object[0], null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
						}
						catch (Exception ex13)

							break;
						}
					}

					{

						break;
					}
					catch (Exception ex17)

							oApp.Msg(Operators.ConcatenateObject("Error in Cycle Manager (OnUserEvent): ", sUserString));
						}
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						break;
					}
				case "BSRestart":
				{
					foreach (Reporter value in cReporters.Values)
					{
						value.Restart();
					}
					break;
				}
				case "BSBatchEnd":
					bEndBatch = true;
					bEoB = true;
					break;
				case "BSStartManualBatch":
					if (bDebugOn)
					{
						oApp.Msg("--Cycle Manager: Configuring next manual cycle");

					{

						}
						catch (Exception ex11)
						{
							ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex11);
							Exception ex12 = ex11;
							if (oApp != null)
							{
								oApp.Msg(Operators.ConcatenateObject("Error in Cycle Manager (OnUserEvent): ", sUserString));
							}
							ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						}
					}
					if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectNotEqual(oApp.AppName, "Aspen Plus", TextCompare: false))
					{
						if (cReporters == null)
						{
							CreateReporter();
						}
						else if (cReporters.Count == 0)
						{
							CreateReporter();
						}
					}
					{
						foreach (Reporter value2 in cReporters.Values)
						{
							value2.Report("EoB");
						}
						break;
					}
				case "BSOpStepEnd":
					bEoS = true;
					break;
				case "BSReportNow":
					if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectNotEqual(oApp.AppName, "Aspen Plus", TextCompare: false))
					{
						if (cReporters == null)
						{
							CreateReporter();
						}
						else if (cReporters.Count == 0)
						{
							CreateReporter();
						}
					}
					{
						foreach (Reporter value3 in cReporters.Values)
						{
							value3.Report();
						}
						break;
					}
				case "BSInitCompletedOK":
					if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectNotEqual(oApp.AppName, "Aspen Plus", TextCompare: false))
					{
						if (cReporters == null)
						{
							CreateReporter();
						}
						else if (cReporters.Count == 0)
						{
							CreateReporter();
						}
					}
					foreach (Reporter value4 in cReporters.Values)
					{
						value4.Report("SoR");
						if (value4.ReportEnabled && value4.ReportStart)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						RestartRun();
					}
					bACMInit = false;
					break;
				case "BSInitCompletedNotOK":
					bACMInit = false;
					break;
				case "BSInitStarted":
					bACMInit = true;
					break;
				case "BSRunEnd":
					if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectNotEqual(oApp.AppName, "Aspen Plus", TextCompare: false))
					{
						if (cReporters == null)
						{
							CreateReporter();
						}
						else if (cReporters.Count == 0)
						{
							CreateReporter();
						}
					}
					{
						foreach (Reporter value5 in cReporters.Values)
						{
							value5.Report("EoR");
						}
						break;
					}
				case "BSCloseDocument":
					bDocumentOpen = false;
					CloseReport();
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex19)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex19);
				Exception ex20 = ex19;
				string text = ex20.Message + "\r\n" + ex20.Source + "\r\n" + ex20.StackTrace;
				Interaction.MsgBox("Unhandled exception during ACM user event handling: \r\n" + text, MsgBoxStyle.Critical, "BatchSep Cycle Manager");
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		void IAspenModelerEvents.OnUserEvent(object sUserString)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in IAspenModelerEvents_OnUserEvent
			this.IAspenModelerEvents_OnUserEvent(sUserString);
		}

		private void RestartRun()
		{
			if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(oSim.State, "Paused", TextCompare: false))
			{
				oSim.Run(false, false);
			}
		}
	}

		protected void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!is_disposed)
			{
				ControlContext = null;
				if (mACMObject != null)
				{
					mACMObject = null;
				}
				if (fsXML != null)
				{
					ReleaseXMLFile();
				}
				sXMLfile = "";
				sReportFile = "";
				sReportPath = "";
				xDoc = null;
				SigFig = 0;
			}
			is_disposed = true;
		}

		~Reporter()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		public void ResetXmlfile(string sFileName)
		{
			string path = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "WorkingFolder", new object[0], null, null, null));
			if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectNotEqual(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "AppName", new object[0], null, null, null), "Aspen Plus", TextCompare: false))
			{
				sXMLfile = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(sFileName) + ".xml");
			}
			else
			{
				sXMLfile = Path.Combine(path, Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(sFileName) + ".xml");
			}
		}

		public void ResetReportFile()
		{
			sReportFile = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportFile", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
		}

		private void InitialiseSettings()
		{
			object instance = null;
			string text = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "WorkingFolder", new object[0], null, null, null));
			if (Conversions.ToBoolean(Operators.AndObject(Operators.CompareObjectNotEqual(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "AppName", new object[0], null, null, null), "Aspen Plus", TextCompare: false), ACMObject != null)))
			{
				instance = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(ACMObject.ActiveDocument);
				sXMLfile = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(instance, null, "Name", new object[0], null, null, null)));
				sXMLfile = Path.ChangeExtension(sXMLfile, "xml");
			}
			else
			{
				sXMLfile = text;
				sXMLfile = Path.Combine(sXMLfile, Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportFile", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Value", new object[0], null, null, null)));
				sXMLfile = Path.ChangeExtension(sXMLfile, "xml");
			}
			bReportEnabled = Conversions.ToBoolean(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportEnabled", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			bReportInputs = Conversions.ToBoolean(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportInputs", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			bReportResults = Conversions.ToBoolean(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportResults", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			sReportFile = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportFile", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			sReportPath = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportFilePath", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			sReportFormat = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportFormat", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			bReportStart = Conversions.ToBoolean(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportStartofRun", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			bReportEnd = Conversions.ToBoolean(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportEndofRun", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			bReportBatch = Conversions.ToBoolean(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportBatchEnd", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			bReportOpStep = Conversions.ToBoolean(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "ReportStepEnd", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			if (Conversions.ToBoolean(Operators.AndObject(Operators.CompareObjectNotEqual(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "AppName", new object[0], null, null, null), "Aspen Plus", TextCompare: false), ACMObject != null)) && ((Operators.CompareString(sReportPath, "", TextCompare: false) == 0) | !Directory.Exists(sReportPath)))
			{
				sReportPath = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(instance, null, "Path", new object[0], null, null, null));
			}
		}

		private XmlDocument GetXMLDoc()
		{
			if (fsXML == null)
			{
				ClaimXMLFile();
			}
			if (xDoc == null)
			{
				xDoc = new XmlDocument();
				if (bDocCreated)
				{
					fsXML.Position = 0L;
					xDoc.Load(fsXML);
					fsXML.Position = 0L;
				}
				else
				{
					XmlDeclaration xmlDeclaration = xDoc.CreateXmlDeclaration("1.0", null, null);
					xDoc.AppendChild(xmlDeclaration);
					XmlNode newChild = BuildRoot();
					xDoc.InsertAfter(newChild, xmlDeclaration);
					XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = xDoc.CreateAttribute("xsi", "noNamespaceSchemaLocation", "http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance");
					string innerText = sXSLLocation + "\\" + sSchemaFile;
					xmlAttribute.InnerText = innerText;
					XmlNode xmlNode = xDoc.SelectSingleNode("BatchSepReportDocument");
					xmlNode.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
					bDocCreated = true;
				}
			}
			return xDoc;
		}

		public void Report(string rPoint = "")
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (!ReportEnabled & (Operators.CompareString(rPoint, "", TextCompare: false) != 0))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				xDoc = GetXMLDoc();
				XmlNode xmlNode = xDoc.SelectSingleNode("BatchSepReportDocument");
				if (!bHeaderAdded)
				{
					XmlNode newChild = BuildHeader();
					xmlNode.AppendChild(newChild);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (!bInputsAdded)
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode2 = BuildInputs();
					XmlNode newChild = xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("Header");
					if (xmlNode2 != null)
					{
						xmlNode.InsertAfter(xmlNode2, newChild);
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (ReportResults)
				{
					switch (rPoint)
					{
					case "SoR":
						if (ReportStart)
						{
							XmlNode xmlNode2 = BuildResults(rPoint);
							if (xmlNode2 != null)
							{
								xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
							}
							NewLateBinding.LateSetComplex(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "AtStartOfRun", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[1] { 0 }, null, null, OptimisticSet: false, RValueBase: true);
							flag = true;
						}
						break;
					case "EoR":
						if (ReportEnd)
						{
							XmlNode xmlNode2 = BuildResults(rPoint);
							if (xmlNode2 != null)
							{
								xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
								flag = true;
							}
						}
						break;
					case "EoB":
						if (ReportStep)
						{
							XmlNode xmlNode2 = BuildResults("EoS");
							if (xmlNode2 != null)
							{
								xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
								flag = true;
							}
						}
						if (ReportBatch)
						{
							XmlNode xmlNode2 = BuildResults(rPoint);
							if (xmlNode2 != null)
							{
								xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
								flag = true;
							}
						}
						break;
					case "EoS":
						if (ReportStep)
						{
							XmlNode xmlNode2 = BuildResults(rPoint);
							if (xmlNode2 != null)
							{
								xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
								flag = true;
							}
						}
						break;
					default:
					{
						XmlNode xmlNode2 = BuildResults(rPoint);
						if (xmlNode2 != null)
						{
							xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
							flag = true;
						}
						break;
					}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					fsReport = ClaimFile(Path.Combine(sReportPath, sReportFile));
					if (fsReport != null)
					{
						TransformFile();
						fsReport.Close();
						fsReport = null;
					}
					if (fsXML != null)
					{
						xDoc.Save(fsXML);
					}
				}
				xDoc = null;
			}
			catch (IOException ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				IOException ex2 = ex;
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "IO Exception" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Error in Reporting (Report): " + ex2.Message }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "                             " + ex2.StackTrace }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			catch (Exception ex3)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex3);
				Exception ex4 = ex3;
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Error in Reporting (Report): " + ex4.Message }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "                             " + ex4.StackTrace }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		private void ClaimXMLFile()
		{
			double num = ((ACMObject == null) ? Conversions.ToDouble(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "CurrentTime", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null)) : Conversions.ToDouble(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ACMObject.Simulation, null, "Time", new object[0], null, null, null)));
			int num2 = Conversions.ToInteger(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "BatchNumber", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			if (fsXML != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (File.Exists(sXMLfile))
				{
					if (num == 0.0)
					{
						if (num2 == 1)
						{
							File.Delete(sXMLfile);
							bDocCreated = false;
							fsXML = File.Open(sXMLfile, FileMode.OpenOrCreate, FileAccess.ReadWrite, FileShare.Read);
						}
						else
						{
							DeleteResultNodes(num2);
							File.Delete(sXMLfile);
							fsXML = File.Open(sXMLfile, FileMode.OpenOrCreate, FileAccess.ReadWrite, FileShare.Read);
							xDoc.Save(fsXML);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					fsXML = File.Create(sXMLfile);
				}
			}
			catch (IOException ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				IOException ex2 = ex;
				Interaction.MsgBox("The file " + sXMLfile + " is in use by another process\r\nPlease close this file and try again. (ClaimXMLFile).", MsgBoxStyle.Critical, "Reporting Error");
				if (ACMObject != null)
				{
					NewLateBinding.LateCall(ACMObject.Simulation, null, "Interrupt", new object[1] { false }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			catch (Exception ex3)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex3);
				Exception ex4 = ex3;
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Exception" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Error in Reporting (ClaimXMLFile): " + ex4.Message }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "                                   " + ex4.StackTrace }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		private void DeleteResultNodes(int batchNum)
		{
			fsXML = File.Open(sXMLfile, FileMode.OpenOrCreate, FileAccess.ReadWrite, FileShare.Read);
			xDoc = GetXMLDoc();
			XmlNode xmlNode = xDoc.SelectSingleNode("BatchSepReportDocument");
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = xmlNode.SelectNodes("Results");
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				if (Conversions.ToDouble(item.SelectSingleNode("ResultPoint/BatchNumber").InnerText) >= (double)batchNum)
				{
					xmlNode.RemoveChild(item);
				}
			}
			fsXML.Close();
		}

		private void ReleaseXMLFile()
		{
			if (fsXML != null)
			{
				fsXML.Close();
			}
			fsXML = null;
		}

		private FileStream ClaimFile(string fName)
		{
			FileStream result;
			try
			{
				FileStream fileStream = File.Open(fName, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.ReadWrite, FileShare.Read);
				result = fileStream;
			}
			catch (DirectoryNotFoundException ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				DirectoryNotFoundException ex2 = ex;
				Interaction.MsgBox("The directory for file " + fName + " does not exist\r\nPlease select another location for this file and try again.", MsgBoxStyle.Critical, "Reporting Error");
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(ACMObject.Simulation, null, "Interrupt", new object[1] { false }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				result = null;
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			catch (IOException ex3)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex3);
				IOException ex4 = ex3;
				Interaction.MsgBox("The file " + fName + " is in use by another process\r\nPlease close this file and try again. (ClaimFile).", MsgBoxStyle.Critical, "Reporting Error");
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(ACMObject.Simulation, null, "Interrupt", new object[1] { false }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				result = null;
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			catch (Exception ex5)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex5);
				Exception ex6 = ex5;
				try
				{
					string fileName = Path.GetFileName(fName);
					string text = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "WorkingFolder", new object[0], null, null, null));
					FileStream fileStream = File.Open(Path.Combine(text, fileName), FileMode.Create, FileAccess.ReadWrite, FileShare.Read);
					NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "  " }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
					NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "WARNING:" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
					NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Unable to create report in " + sReportPath + "." }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
					NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Report will be created in " + text + "." }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
					sReportPath = text;
					result = fileStream;
					ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
				}
				catch (Exception ex7)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex7);
					Exception ex8 = ex7;
					NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Exception" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);

					result = null;
					ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool ReleaseFile(string fname)
		{
			if (fsReport != null)
			{
				fsReport.Close();
				fsReport = null;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void Restart()
		{
			ReleaseXMLFile();
			if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "BatchNumber", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null), 1, TextCompare: false))
			{
				bHeaderAdded = false;
				bInputsAdded = false;
			}
		}

		private void TransformFile()
		{
			XmlUrlResolver documentResolver = new XmlUrlResolver();
			XslCompiledTransform xslCompiledTransform = new XslCompiledTransform();
			XmlNodeReader input = new XmlNodeReader(xDoc);
			try
			{
				switch (sReportFormat)
				{
				case "TXT":
				{
					XmlTextWriter xmlTextWriter = new XmlTextWriter(fsReport, null);
					string stylesheetUri = sXSLLocation + "\\" + sTXTFile;
					xslCompiledTransform.Load(stylesheetUri);
					xslCompiledTransform.Transform(input, null, xmlTextWriter, documentResolver);
					break;
				}
				case "RTF":
				{
					XmlTextWriter xmlTextWriter = new XmlTextWriter(fsReport, null);
					string stylesheetUri = sXSLLocation + "\\" + sRTFFile;
					xslCompiledTransform.Load(stylesheetUri);
					xslCompiledTransform.Transform(input, null, xmlTextWriter, documentResolver);
					xmlTextWriter.Close();
					break;
				}
				case "CSV":
				{
					XmlTextWriter xmlTextWriter = new XmlTextWriter(fsReport, null);
					string stylesheetUri = sXSLLocation + "\\" + sCSVFile;
					xslCompiledTransform.Load(stylesheetUri);
					xslCompiledTransform.Transform(input, null, xmlTextWriter, documentResolver);
					xmlTextWriter.Close();
					break;
				}
				case "HTML":
				{
					XmlTextWriter xmlTextWriter = new XmlTextWriter(fsReport, null);
					string stylesheetUri = sXSLLocation + "\\" + sHTMLFile;
					xslCompiledTransform.Load(stylesheetUri);
					xslCompiledTransform.Transform(input, null, xmlTextWriter, documentResolver);
					xmlTextWriter.Close();
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			catch (IOException ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				IOException ex2 = ex;
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "IO Exception" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Error in Reporting (TransformFile): " + ex2.Message }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "                                    " + ex2.StackTrace }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			catch (XsltException ex3)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex3);
				XsltException ex4 = ex3;
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "XSLT Exception" }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Error in Reporting (TransformFile): " + ex4.Message }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "                                    " + ex4.StackTrace }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			catch (Exception ex5)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex5);
				Exception ex6 = ex5;
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "Error in Reporting (TransformFile): " + ex6.Message }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Msg", new object[1] { "                                    " + ex6.StackTrace }, null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);

			}
		}

			return xmlNode;
		}

		{

			{

		}

		private XmlNode BuildDataElement(string text, object att, bool units = false)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = xDoc.CreateElement("DataElement");
			XmlNode xmlNode2 = xDoc.CreateElement("Text");
			XmlNode xmlNode3 = xDoc.CreateElement("Value");
			XmlNode xmlNode4 = xDoc.CreateElement("Units");
			xmlNode2.InnerText = text;
			xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
			if (units)
			{
				xmlNode3.InnerText = FormatValue(Conversions.ToDouble(NewLateBinding.LateGet(att, null, "value", new object[1] { "CurrentUnits" }, null, null, null)));
				xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
				xmlNode4.InnerText = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(att, null, "units", new object[0], null, null, null));
				xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode4);
			}
			else if (NewLateBinding.LateGet(att, null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null) is double)
			{
				xmlNode3.InnerText = FormatValue(Conversions.ToDouble(NewLateBinding.LateGet(att, null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null)));
				xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
			}
			else
			{
				xmlNode3.InnerText = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(att, null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
				xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
			}
			return xmlNode;
		}

		}

		private XmlNode FindNode(string name, string nodeType, ref XmlNode head)

		private void BuildComponentsTable(ref XmlNode tab, string route, string col2Title, bool units)
		{

			xmlNode2.AppendChild(newChild);
			if (units)
			{
				newChild = BuildRowDataElement(3, "Units", "Units");
				xmlNode2.AppendChild(newChild);
			}

			{
				string text = Conversions.ToString(item);
				xmlNode2 = xDoc.CreateElement("Row");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);

				string text2 = route + "(\"" + text + "\")";

				bool[] array2;

				if (array2[0])
				{
					text2 = (string)Conversions.ChangeType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(array[0]), typeof(string));
				}
				object objectValue2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(obj);
				newChild = ((!units) ? 					xmlNode2.AppendChild(newChild);
				}
			}
			SetWidth(xmlNode);
		}

		private XmlNode BuildHeader()
		{
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			XmlNode result;
			try
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = xDoc.CreateElement("Header");
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = xDoc.CreateElement("Title");
				if (ACMObject != null)
				{
					xmlNode2.InnerText = Conversions.ToString(Operators.ConcatenateObject("Report for ", NewLateBinding.LateGet(ACMObject.ActiveDocument, null, "Name", new object[0], null, null, null)));
				}
				else
				{
					xmlNode2.InnerText = Conversions.ToString(Operators.ConcatenateObject("Report for ", NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "BlockID", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null)));
				}
				xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
				xmlNode2 = xDoc.CreateElement("InputFilename");
				if (ACMObject != null)
				{
					xmlNode2.InnerText = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ACMObject.ActiveDocument, null, "FullName", new object[0], null, null, null));
				}

		}

		private void BuildFittingCases(ref XmlNode head)
		{
			object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "FittingCasesSet", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			BuildNodes(ref head, "FittingCasesSet", "Fitting Case", "FittingCasesValid");
			int num = Conversions.ToInteger(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue, null, "count", new object[0], null, null, null));
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i = checked(i + 1))
			{
				string text = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(objectValue, new object[1] { i }, null));
				XmlNode xmlNode = FindNode(text, "Fitting Case", ref head);
				XmlNode tab = BuildTab("Configure");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulateConfigureTab(ref tab, text);
				tab = BuildTab("Estimated Parameters");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulateEstimatedParametersTab(ref tab, text);
				tab = BuildTab("Predicted vs Measured");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulatePredictedVsMeasuredTab(ref tab, text);
			}
		}

		private void PopulateConfigureTab(ref XmlNode tab, string FittingCaseName)

		private void BuildControllers(ref XmlNode head)
		{
			object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "UserControllers", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			BuildNodes(ref head, "UserControllers", "Controller", "ControllersValid");
			int num = Conversions.ToInteger(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue, null, "count", new object[0], null, null, null));
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i = checked(i + 1))
			{
				string text = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(objectValue, new object[1] { i }, null));
				XmlNode xmlNode = FindNode(text, "Controller", ref head);
				XmlNode tab = BuildTab("Connections");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulateConnections(ref tab, text);
				tab = BuildTab("Parameters");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulateParameters(ref tab, text);
			}
		}

		private void PopulateConnections(ref XmlNode tab, string cName)
		{
			object controlContext = ControlContext;
			object[] obj = new object[1] { cName };

			bool[] obj2 = new bool[1] { true };
			bool[] array2 = obj2;

			if (array2[0])
			{

			object objectValue3 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue, null, "PVType", new object[0], null, null, null));

			newChild = BuildDataElement("Process variable set point", RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue3), units: true);
			tab.AppendChild(newChild);
			if (Regex.IsMatch(text, "Stage"))
			{
				objectValue3 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue, null, "PVStage", new object[0], null, null, null));
				newChild = BuildDataElement("Process variable stage", RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue3));
				tab.AppendChild(newChild);
			}
			if (Regex.IsMatch(text, "fraction") | ((Operators.CompareString(text, "Reflux ratio", TextCompare: false) == 0) & (Operators.CompareString(left, "Vapor-Liquid", TextCompare: false) != 0)))
			{
				objectValue3 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue, null, "PVPhase", new object[0], null, null, null));
				newChild = BuildDataElement("Process variable phase", RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue3));

			{

				newChild = BuildDataElement("Process variable component", RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue3));

			tab.AppendChild(newChild);

			{
			case "Charge stream":

			newChild = BuildDataElement("Output variable type", RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue3));

		}
