		private XmlNode BuildResults(string rPoint)
		{
			string left = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "configuration", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			XmlNode result;
			try
			{
				if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectNotEqual(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "appname", new object[0], null, null, null), "Aspen Plus", TextCompare: false))
				{
					NewLateBinding.LateCall(ControlContext, null, "UpdateVariables", new object[0], null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				}
				XmlNode xmlNode = xDoc.CreateElement("Results");
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = BuildResultPoint(rPoint);
				if (xmlNode2 == null)
				{
					result = null;
				}
				else
				{
					xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
					if (Operators.CompareString(xmlNode2.Attributes["HasResults"].InnerText, "True", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						XmlNode xmlNode3;
						if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectGreater(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "BatchNumber", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null), 1.0, TextCompare: false))
						{
							xmlNode3 = BuildMultipleBatchInitialCharge();
							if (xmlNode3 != null)
							{
								xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
							}
						}
						xmlNode3 = BuildChargeStreamResults();
						if (xmlNode3 != null)
						{
							xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
						}
						xmlNode3 = BuildPotResults();
						xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
						if (Operators.CompareString(left, "Pot", TextCompare: false) != 0)
						{
							xmlNode3 = BuildProfileResults();
							xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
							xmlNode3 = BuildCondenserResults();
							xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
							if (Regex.IsMatch(Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Condenser", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "condensertype", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null)), "2nd condenser"))
							{
								xmlNode3 = BuildCondenser2Results();
								xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
							}
							xmlNode3 = BuildRefluxResults();
							if (xmlNode3 != null)
							{
								xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
							}
							xmlNode3 = BuildDistillateResults();
							xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
							xmlNode3 = BuildHoldupSummaryResults();
							xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
						}
						xmlNode3 = BuildControllerResults();
						if (xmlNode3 != null)
						{
							xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
						}
						xmlNode3 = BuildOpStepResults();
						if (xmlNode3 != null)
						{
							xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
						}
						xmlNode3 = BuildMultipleBatchResults();
						xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode3);
					}
					result = xmlNode;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);

		private XmlNode BuildPotResults()
		{
			string left = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "configuration", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			string left2 = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Pot", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "ModelDetail", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));
			XmlNode xmlNode = BuildResultsForm("Pot Results");
			if ((Operators.CompareString(left, "Pot", TextCompare: false) == 0) & (Operators.CompareString(left2, "Shortcut", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				XmlNode tab = BuildTab("Main");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulatePotResultsMainTab(ref tab);
			}
			else
			{
				XmlNode tab = BuildTab("Holdup");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulateHoldupTab(ref tab);
				tab = BuildTab("Heat transfer summary");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulateHeatTransferSummaryTab(ref tab);
				tab = BuildTab("Jacket");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulateJacketTab(ref tab);
				tab = BuildTab("Coils");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulateCoilsTab(ref tab);
				tab = BuildTab("Heat exchanger");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
				PopulateExtHXTab(ref tab);
			}
			return xmlNode;
		}

		private void PopulatePotResultsMainTab(ref XmlNode tab)
		{
			object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Pot", new object[0], null, null, null));
			string left = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "validphases", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[0], null, null, null));

			newChild = BuildDataElement("Temperature", RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue2), units: true);
			tab.AppendChild(newChild);

		private XmlNode BuildProfileResults()
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = BuildResultsForm("Profile Results");
			XmlNode tab = BuildTab("TPFQ");
			xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
			PopulateTPFQTab(ref tab);
			tab = BuildTab("Reactions");
			xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
			PopulateReactionTab(ref tab);
			tab = BuildTab("Composition");
			xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
			PopulateCompositionTab(ref tab);
			tab = BuildTab("Holdup");
			xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
			PopulateHoldupResultsTab(ref tab);
			tab = BuildTab("Properties");
			xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
			PopulatePropertiesTab(ref tab);
			return xmlNode;
		}

		private XmlNode BuildCondenserResults()
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = BuildResultsForm("Condenser Results");
			XmlNode tab = BuildTab("Main");
			xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
			PopulateCondenserMainTab(ref tab);
			tab = BuildTab("Cooling");
			xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
			PopulateCoolingTab(ref tab);
			tab = BuildTab("Composition");
			xmlNode.AppendChild(tab);
			PopulateCondenserCompositionTab(ref tab);
			return xmlNode;
		}

		{

				PopulateSummaryDistillateTab(ref tab);
			}
			if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectGreater(NewLateBinding.LateGet(objectValue, null, "count", new object[0], null, null, null), 0, TextCompare: false))
			{
				tab = BuildTab("Side Draws");

				PopulateSummarySideDrawsTab(ref tab);
			}
			return xmlNode;
		}

			tab.AppendChild(newChild);
			double value2 = Conversions.ToDouble(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "CurrentTotalTime", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "value", new object[1] { "CurrentUnits" }, null, null, null));
			units = Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "CurrentTotalTime", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "units", new object[1] { "CurrentUnits" }, null, null, null));
			newChild = BuildDataElement("Time from start of first batch", FormatValue(value2), units);
			tab.AppendChild(newChild);
		}

		private string FormatValue(double value)
		{
			checked
			{
				string style = new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), 1) + "." + new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), Math.Max(SigFig - 1, 0)) + "e+000";
				double num = Convert.ToDouble(Strings.Format(value, style));
				if (Math.Abs(num) < 1E-09)
				{
					return "0";
				}
				double num2 = Math.Abs(num);
				if (num2 == 0.0)
				{
					return Strings.Format(num, "0.0");
				}
				if (num2 < 0.001)
				{
					return Strings.Format(num, "#." + new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), Math.Max(SigFig - 1, 0)) + "e+00");
				}
				if (num2 < 1.0)
				{
					return Strings.Format(num, "0." + new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), SigFig));
				}
				if (num2 < 10.0)
				{
					return Strings.Format(num, "#." + new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), Math.Max(SigFig - 1, 0)));
				}
				if (num2 < 100.0)
				{
					return Strings.Format(num, "##." + new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), Math.Max(SigFig - 2, 0)));
				}
				if (num2 < 1000.0)
				{
					return Strings.Format(num, "###." + new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), Math.Max(SigFig - 3, 0)));
				}
				if (num2 < 10000.0)
				{
					return Strings.Format(num, "####." + new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), Math.Max(SigFig - 4, 0)));
				}
				if (num2 < 100000.0)
				{
					return Strings.Format(num, "#####." + new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), Math.Max(SigFig - 5, 0)));
				}
				return Strings.Format(num, "#." + new string(Convert.ToChar("#"), Math.Max(SigFig - 1, 0)) + "e+00");
			}
		}

		private void CacheSigFig()
		{
			if (ACMObject == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				string[] array = Regex.Split(Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(ControlContext, null, "Application", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "Version", new object[0], null, null, null)), "\\.");
				RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("Software\\AspenTech\\AMSystem\\" + array[0] + "." + array[1] + "\\Settings\\Preferences", writable: false);
				SigFig = Math.Max(Conversions.ToInteger(registryKey.GetValue("SigFig")), 1);
				if (Conversions.ToInteger(registryKey.GetValue("SigFig")) < 1)
				{
					SigFig = 6;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				SigFig = 6;
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		private void SetWidth(XmlNode table)
		{
			int count = table.SelectNodes("HeaderRow/RowDataElement").Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = count;
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i = checked(i + 1))
			{
				string xpath = "*/RowDataElement[@ColNumber='" + Conversions.ToString(i) + "']";
				XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = table.SelectNodes(xpath);
				if (xmlNodeList.Count != 0)
				{
					int num2 = FindLongestValue(xmlNodeList);
					int num3 = FindTwipLength(xmlNodeList);
					xpath = "HeaderRow/RowDataElement[@ColNumber='" + Conversions.ToString(i) + "']";
					XmlNode xmlNode = table.SelectSingleNode(xpath);
					XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = xDoc.CreateAttribute("Width");
					xmlAttribute.InnerText = num2.ToString();
					xmlNode.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
					xmlAttribute = xDoc.CreateAttribute("TwipWidth");
					xmlAttribute.InnerText = num3.ToString();
					xmlNode.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
				}
			}
		}

		private int TwipsToPixels(int Twips, bool IsHorizontal)
		{
			Graphics graphics = Graphics.FromHwnd(IntPtr.Zero);
			IntPtr hdc = graphics.GetHdc();
			int hdc2 = hdc.ToInt32();
			if (IsHorizontal)
			{
				PixelsPerInch = GetDeviceCaps(hdc2, 88);
			}
			else
			{
				PixelsPerInch = GetDeviceCaps(hdc2, 90);
			}
			graphics.ReleaseHdc(hdc);
			return checked((int)Math.Round((double)Twips / 1440.0 * (double)PixelsPerInch));
		}

		private int PixelsToTwips(int Pixels, bool IsHorizontal)
		{
			Graphics graphics = Graphics.FromHwnd(IntPtr.Zero);
			IntPtr hdc = graphics.GetHdc();
			int hdc2 = hdc.ToInt32();
			if (IsHorizontal)
			{
				PixelsPerInch = GetDeviceCaps(hdc2, 88);
			}
			else
			{
				PixelsPerInch = GetDeviceCaps(hdc2, 90);
			}
			graphics.ReleaseHdc(hdc);
			double num = (double)Pixels / (double)PixelsPerInch;
			return checked((int)Math.Round(num * 1440.0));
		}

		private int FindTwipLength(XmlNodeList nodes)
		{
			int num = 0;
			dlgFontMeasure dlgFontMeasure2 = new dlgFontMeasure();
			Graphics graphics = dlgFontMeasure2.CreateGraphics();
			foreach (XmlNode node in nodes)
			{
				num = checked((int)Math.Round(Math.Max(num, graphics.MeasureString(node.InnerText + "X", theFont).Width)));
			}
			num = PixelsToTwips(num, IsHorizontal: true);
			dlgFontMeasure2.Dispose();
			graphics.Dispose();
			return num;
		}

		private int FindLongestValue(XmlNodeList nodes)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode node in nodes)
			{
				num = Math.Max(num, node.InnerText.Length);
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
